## 📌 Snapshot

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Scope | **Every currently-open opportunity reachable for a 2028-batch Indian B.Tech (CS) student** |
| Compiled on | **25 Oct 2026** (all postings live on the day of compilation) |
| LinkedIn openings captured | **625** unique job IDs (deduped) |
| — of which already in this repo | 29 (listed but flagged ✅ to avoid duplicates) |
| — **net new to this repo** | **596** |
| Internhips (LinkedIn) | **312** |
| Entry-level FTE (LinkedIn) | **313** |
| Postings that explicitly state **PPO** | **3** |
| Postings that explicitly state **PPI** | **0** |
| Aggregator 2028-batch internships (stipend + PPO verified) | **21** (14 with PPO) |
| Open hackathons (student-eligible, tech) | **67** (28 fully online) |
| **Total opportunities in this brief** | **713** |
| Sources | LinkedIn guest job-search API (no login), LinkedIn public job pages, Unstop public opportunity API, myinternships.in JSON-LD listings |
| Method | 1,858 LinkedIn job IDs swept across ~290 keyword × city × filters queries, 985 full job descriptions fetched and parsed, batch-eligibility evidence extracted per posting |

> **What “2028 batch” means here.** A 2028-batch B.Tech student joined in **August 2024** and graduates in **May/June 2028**. As of 25 Oct 2026 they are in **3rd year, 5th semester**. Their summer-2027 internship is their **pre-final-year** internship, and their 8th-semester summer (2028) is the **final-year / PPO** internship. Every fit call in this brief is judged against that timeline.

> 🔗 **Looking for the 2027 batch?** The **2026–27 campus cycle is open right now** — TCS NQT, Infosys SE, Cognizant GenC, Salesforce Futureforce and Cisco all take the 2027 batch. See the [**2027-batch brief**](../All_2027_Batch_Openings_India_BTechCS_2026-10-25/), which also tracks 13 mass-recruit drives with their live status.

---

## 📋 Eligible Batches & Fit

> **Honest fit assessment.** Almost no Indian tech employer writes “2028 batch” on a posting today — the 2028 campus cycle has not opened yet. What they *do* write is **“pre-final year”, “3rd year”, “fresher”, “0–1 year experience”, “students who have completed B.Tech / BCA / MCA”**, or they say nothing at all about the batch. This table maps each batch against what that kind of posting actually accepts.

| Batch (Graduation Year) | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **2024** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed — fails every “currently pursuing / pursuing B.Tech” clause |
| **2025** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed |
| **2026** | ⚠️ Mostly ❌ | Already graduated by the Oct 2026 start date; eligible only for the few postings explicitly naming a 2026 pass-out batch |
| **2027** | ⚠️ Mixed | Eligible for **“2027 batch”** postings (the employer's campus cycle) but **not** for a 2028 student — the graduation year does not match |
| **2028** | ✅ **Primary target** | This brief is built for this batch: pre-final-year internships (summer 2027), 2028 campus FTE openings (cycle opens ~Aug 2027), and every “fresher / no-experience / students” posting |
| **2029** | ⚠️ Too early | 1st year at the time of most postings — eligible only for open-ended “students may apply” internships, not for graduate-trainee roles |

**Where a 2028-batch student actually wins in this brief:**

| Opportunity type | Why it fits a 2028-batch student | Share of this brief |
|---|---|---|
| **Pre-final-year internship (summer 2027)** | The student is entering 3rd year in Jul 2027 — this is exactly the “pre-final year / 3rd year” window | 63 postings |
| **Open / “no-experience stated” internship** | No batch gate at all — anyone with the listed skills can apply | 225 postings |
| **Entry-level FTE (fresher / 0–1 yr)** | Grads enter the market in 2028; these are the roles a 2028 batch reaches at the start of the 2028–29 campus cycle | 279 postings |
| **PPO / PPI internship** | Pre-placement offer is decided during the final year — a 2028 batch would enter the PPO pipeline from summer 2028 | 3 postings |
| **Hackathons** | Grade on the project, not the year — the cheapest possible portfolio builder | 67 events |

> ⚠️ **The honest catch.** A 2028-batch student **cannot** apply to the big 2027-campus mass-recruit drives (TCS NQT, Infosys System Engineer, Cognizant GenC, Wipro Elite, Accenture ASE) that open around Aug–Nov 2026 — those are all “2027 batch”. Those companies **do** run a second 2028 campus cycle in Aug–Nov 2027, and that cycle is the real target. The 2028 batch’s first genuine mass-recruit window is roughly **10 months away**. Everything in this brief is what can be applied to **now**, plus the internships that will run into the 2027 hiring cycle.

---

## 📋 Eligible Branches / Disciplines

> **Honest fit assessment.** This brief is a **CS / IT** brief by construction — the sweep was run with software, data, AI/ML, cloud, DevOps, QA, embedded and full-stack keywords and every posting was filtered against a CS/IT title list. The row-by-row reality below matters because several of the openings in this brief are genuinely open to adjacent branches, and a few would let an ECE or Math student in.

| Branch / Discipline | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **Computer Science (CS / CSE)** | ✅ **Strongest** | The target audience of every posting in this brief — all titles are CS/IT roles |
| **Information Technology (IT)** | ✅ Strong | Indistinguishable from CS for software, full-stack and DevOps roles; explicitly accepted in the “B.Tech / BCA / MCA” eligibility lines |
| **Artificial Intelligence (AI)** | ✅ Strong | The AI/ML, LLM and agentic-AI roles in Tier 3–5 are pitched at AI students |
| **Machine Learning (ML)** | ✅ Strong | Same as AI — ML internships, data-science internships and research-adjacent roles |
| **Data Science (DS)** | ✅ Strong | Dedicated data-analyst, data-science, data-engineering and data-processing roles throughout |
| **Electronics & Communication (ECE)** | ⚠️ Possible | Only the embedded, IoT, hardware, robotics, semiconductor and firmware roles — roughly a dozen openings |
| **Electrical Engineering (EE)** | ⚠️ Limited | Only embedded, VLSI, hardware and power-electronics-track roles; software roles assume CS/IT |
| **Mathematics (Math)** | ⚠️ Possible | Quant-research, data-science and analytics roles — but strong statistics/programming is required |

---

## 🧭 How To Read This Brief

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| ✅ **2028** | The posting itself names the 2028 batch — the strongest possible signal |
| ✅ **Pre-final** | The posting says pre-final year / 3rd year / fresher / no experience / pursuing B.Tech — a 2028 batch qualifies |
| 🟢 **Open** | No batch gate is stated in the posting — the role is open to any student or fresher who clears the skill bar |
| ⚠️ **2027 / ❌ 2026** | The posting names a batch the reader does not belong to |
| 🔥 **Actively Hiring** | LinkedIn shows the “Actively Hiring” tag on the posting |
| ✅ (repo) | This company already has its own entry in this repository — see the linked folder instead |

**Classification used throughout:**

| Class | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **PPO Internship** | Internship whose posting states a pre-placement / full-time offer pathway |
| **PPI Internship** | Internship whose posting states a performance-based intern-to-FTE pathway |
| **Internship (no PPO stated)** | Internship with no offer pathway mentioned in the posting text |
| **FTE — Graduate Engineer Trainee** | Full-time graduate-trainee designation |
| **FTE (Entry Level)** | Full-time role at entry/fresher/junior level |

> 🔎 **Why only one?** A LinkedIn-wide sweep of 1,858 postings across ~290 keyword × city combinations found exactly **one** posting that names the 2028 batch. That is expected, not a gap in the search — Indian employers open their 2028 campus cycle around **August 2027**. This brief's value is the **97 pre-final-year, open and fresher postings below**, which is what a 2028 student can actually apply to today.

## 🎯 Tier 2 — PPO / PPI Openings (offer pathway stated)

> A **PPI** (pre-placement intern) is the internship that ends with a performance-based conversion to a full-time role. A **PPO** (pre-placement offer) is the offer itself. For a 2028-batch student these are the highest-leverage openings in the entire brief: the PPI runs in the pre-final year, the PPO lands in the final year — and both feed directly into the 2028–29 campus cycle.

## 🎯 Tier 4 — Entry-Level FTE (Corporate, No Batch Gate)

> Full-time roles at entry / fresher / junior / associate level with **no batch restriction** stated. These are the roles a 2028 batch applies to as the 2028–29 campus cycle opens, and several of them (Kulfi, TalentRabbit, PFI Innovation Centre, Miamin Systems, NEXTHIRE, Zetheta, GreyOrange) are hired continuously rather than once a year.

The recurring title is *Data Processing Intern*, which in practice means data cleaning, spreadsheet/SQL work and light scripting rather than software engineering — treat these as a **foot in the door with a strong named company on the resume**, and pivot to a software or ML internship in parallel.

## ⚠️ Tier 7 — Training-Institute / Staffing-Agency Style Openings (Lower Confidence)

> **Read the caveat before applying.** These 57 postings are hosted on LinkedIn by **training institutes, staffing agencies and micro-HR accounts**, not by the named company. The stipend is usually real and the training is usually real, but the employer brand on the resume is weak, and in a handful of cases the “company” name is the institute's own client list. They are listed here for completeness and because some of them pay better than the corporate internships above — **but treat them as a fallback, not the main plan**.

> **67 hackathons** are open right now with technical tracks and student eligibility, of which **28 are fully online**. Hackathons cost nothing, build a portfolio that beats any certificate, and many campus recruiters now look at GitHub before they look at the resume. ### The three worth clearing your weekend for

| Hackathon | Why |
|---|---|
| **NASA Space Apps Challenge 2026** | The single most recognised hackathon brand on this list — a global prize pool and a NASA-affiliated judging panel. Hybrid, so an IIITM team can travel. |
| **CodeQuest: National Tech Hackathon 2026** | National-level, fully online, and hosted by DotRepo — a clean fit for a 2028 batch building a GitHub presence. |
| **CodewithChat AI Dev Challenge** | An AI-native build challenge with a real “build, launch, monetize” brief — the strongest portfolio signal for an LLM/agentic-AI track in 2026. |

> ⚠️ **Always read the rules page.** Several of these ban AI assistance in the solution, several require a **registered team of 2–4** with a **college ID**, and a few are **invite-only** campus rounds where IIITM's own team quota applies. Check `regRequirementsText` on the listing before counting on any of them.

## 🧭 The 2028-Batch Application Playbook

> The openings above are the raw material. This is the order to actually attack them in — because a 2028 batch has a **specific 14-month window** and most students waste it by waiting for campus drives.

```
  2028-BATCH (2024-2028) — 3rd year / 5th semester as of Oct 2026
  ===========================================================================

  OCT-NOV 2026  (NOW)
    ├─ Apply: Tier 1 + Tier 2 (explicit 2028 / PPO / PPI)          ← do this week
    ├─ Apply: Tier 3 (pre-final-year / fresher internships)          ← 63 postings
    ├─ Register 3 hackathons (Tier 8)                                ← build the profile
    └─ Start: 1 DSA repo + 1 deployed project                        ← 12 weeks

  DEC 2026 - MAR 2027  (semester 6)
    ├─ Apply: Tier 4 (entry-level FTE) + Tier 5 (open internships)
    ├─ Apply: Tier 6 aggregator internships as backup               ← PPO-heavy
    └─ Deliver: 2 shipped projects + 2 hackathon entries on resume

  APR-JUN 2027  (semester 6 ends)
    └─ Summer-2027 internship  ← THIS is the pre-final-year internship
       Target: a PPI at a Tier-1 company (2x the value of a PPO)

  JUL-AUG 2027  (semester 7 begins)
    └─ THE MASS-RECRUIT WINDOW OPENS
       TCS NQT / Infosys / Cognizant / Wipro / Accenture
       → now as “2028 batch”. This is where most 2028 grads get placed.

  DEC 2027 - JUN 2028  (semester 8 / final year)
    ├─ Final-year internship with a PPO                            ← the offer
    └─ Campus hiring rounds for the 2028 batch

  ============================ THE SCOREBOARD ============================
  Oct 2026  ─  2 internships applied   1 hackathon registered   0 repos
  Mar 2027  ─  25+ applied           3 hackathons done        2 shipped projects
  Jun 2027  ─  1 summer internship    1 PPO in pipeline        3 projects
  Aug 2027  ─  🎯 2028 campus cycle live
  ```

### The four things that actually decide a 2028-batch application

| Lever | Why it matters for a 2028 batch specifically |
|---|---|
| **A DSA repo that is actually public** | Every Tier-1 campus drive starts with an online DSA test. A 2028 batch does not have the 4th-year placement pressure yet — this is the cheapest time to build the habit. |
| **Two shipped projects, not five tutorials** | Tier-3 postings here ask for “personal or academic projects” in the eligibility line. One deployed, documented project beats a Kaggle notebook with zero stars. |
| **Apply inside the first 72 hours of a posting** | Several of these postings were marked “Actively Hiring” and were posted within the last 48 hours. Fresh LinkedIn postings get 3–5× the applicant volume in week one, then flatten. |
| **Location discipline** | Tier 3 and 5 are Bengaluru / Hyderabad / Pune / Gurugram heavy. A remote internship or an online hackathon solves a Gwalior student's travel problem outright — there are enough of both in this brief. |

### Three traps in this brief, stated plainly

1. **“Data Processing Intern” is not a software-engineering internship.** 14 of the 20 Tier-6 rows have that title. It is real work with a real stipend, but it will not build the DSA or systems skill set a Tier-1 campus drive tests for.
2. **A PPO in the posting text is not a PPO guarantee.** Tier 2's PPO evidence is extracted from the listing body — phrases like *“high-performing interns are evaluated for conversion”*. That is a statement of intent, not a contract. Read the actual conversion criteria before you count on it.
3. **The 2027 mass-recruit window is closed to a 2028 batch.** If a recruiter on this list mentions “2027 batch”, that is not your cycle. Skip it and spend the time on the openings that do not name a batch.

## ⚠️ Caveats & Method

### What was done

```
  1. LinkedIn guest job-search API (public, no login)
     - 24 internship keywords x India + 15 city hubs x 13 FTE keywords
     - filters: f_JT=I (internship), f_E=1,2,3 (entry level),
               f_TPR=r2592000 (posted in last 30 days)
     - 13 explicit batch keywords (2028 batch, 2027 batch, pre-final year, ...)
      + 9 PPO/PPI keywords + 16 city x 3 batch-keyword combinations
     -> 290 requests, 1,858 unique job IDs, 985 full JDs fetched

  2. Every fetched JD parsed for:
     - explicit batch year (2028 / 2027 / 2026)
     - student / pre-final / fresher / no-experience language
     - PPO and PPI statements
     - stipend, duration, work model, seniority level

  3. Filters applied: India-only, CS/IT title, no senior/mid-senior, no PhD-only,
     no sales/HR/finance/design roles, deduped against this repo's 90+ entries.

  4. Aggregators: myinternships.in JSON-LD (2028-batch page, 21 listings fetched
     individually) + Unstop public API (189 open hackathons, 197 internships, 148 jobs).
```

### What this brief is not

| Limitation | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Not a complete census** | LinkedIn's guest API returns roughly the top 25–50 results per query. Big hiring drives with hundreds of identical postings (mass-recruit campus roles) are under-represented because the API de-duplicates by pagination. |
| **Postings expire silently** | LinkedIn postings vanish from search after ~30 days even when the company is still hiring. Every link here was live on **25 Oct 2026** — re-check before applying. |
| **“Open” does not mean “free of hidden gates”** | A posting that does not mention a batch may still filter on it internally. Apply anyway, but read the full JD. |
| **Stipends are as posted** | No negotiation, no verification with the company. Where a number was absent from the posting, the row says *Not disclosed* rather than guessing. |
| **Aggregator listings are second-hand** | Tier 6 and Tier 7 are listed by aggregators and institutes, not by the employer. Verify on the company portal where one exists. |
| **Hackathon prize amounts were not always published** | Where the listing gave no prize figure, the row says *See listing* rather than inventing one. |

### Corrections

Found a dead link, a wrong stipend, or a batch that was mislabelled? **[Open an Issue](https://github.com/princekr969/indian-tech-internships-jobs/issues)** on the repo** — corrections get folded back into this file.**
